{"forms":[{"form":"more Promethean","tags":["comparative"]},{"form":"most Promethean","tags":["superlative"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"adj","senses":[{"glosses":["Of or pertaining to Prometheus, a demigod in Greek mythology who created mortals from clay and stole fire from Zeus to give to them, for which Zeus punished him by chaining him to a rock and having an eagle feed on his liver which grew back each night; he was later rescued by Heracles."]},{"glosses":["Of or pertaining to Prometheus, a demigod in Greek mythology who created mortals from clay and stole fire from Zeus to give to them, for which Zeus punished him by chaining him to a rock and having an eagle feed on his liver which grew back each night; he was later rescued by Heracles.","Boldly creative, inventive, or original; skilful, talented; also, recklessly daring; audacious."]},{"glosses":["Of or pertaining to Prometheus, a demigod in Greek mythology who created mortals from clay and stole fire from Zeus to give to them, for which Zeus punished him by chaining him to a rock and having an eagle feed on his liver which grew back each night; he was later rescued by Heracles.","Of a Romantic literary hero: defying traditional moral categories; rebelling against a larger order; persecuted but dauntless."]},{"glosses":["Of or pertaining to the promethea silkmoth (Callosamia promethea)."],"tags":["rare"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/pɹəˈmiː.θɪ.ən/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/pɹəˈmi.θi.ən/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/pɹoʊ-/","tags":["General-American"]}],"upstream_line":300313,"upstream_offset":1499420489,"upstream_record_sha256":"8fc8f1887ccabcb11e123056a4d961d73ffef32ed10df3ab79bf0e82dd128ff8","word":"Promethean"}

This type of match was superseded by the friction match which was lit by rubbing against a rough surface."],"tags":["also","attributive","historical"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/pɹəˈmiː.θɪ.ən/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/pɹəˈmi.θi.ən/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/pɹoʊ-/","tags":["General-American"]}],"upstream_line":300314,"upstream_offset":1499435594,"upstream_record_sha256":"1565341082de87c059922b34b3e72fafc2d69ce010cc2bae87863b4b53e746fb","word":"Promethean"}

{"lang_code":"en","pos":"adj","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"x²-1 is an antipalindromic polynomial.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Being equivalent to the object whose constituents or parameters are reversed in order and mapped by some involution, particularly the inversion operator of a quasigroup.","Of a polynomial, being equivalent to the polynomial with reversed and additively inverted coefficients: ∑ᵢ₌₀ⁿa_ixⁱ is antipalindromic iff ∑ᵢ₌₀ⁿa_ixⁱ=∑ᵢ₌₀ⁿ-a_n-ixⁱ⟺a_i=-a_n-i."],"tags":["not-comparable"],"topics":["mathematics","sciences"]},{"examples":[{"text":"1728#95;#123;10#125; is an antipalindromic number with respect to base 10.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Being equivalent to the object whose constituents or parameters are reversed in order and mapped by some involution, particularly the inversion operator of a quasigroup.","Of a natural number, with respect to base b, being equivalent to the natural number whose digits are reversed and subtracted from b-1: ∑ᵢ₌₀ⁿa_ibⁱ is antipalindromic iff ∑ᵢ₌₀ⁿa_ibⁱ=∑ᵢ₌₀ⁿ(b-1-a_n-i)bⁱ⟺a_i=b-1-a_n-i."],"tags":["not-comparable"],"topics":["mathematics","sciences"]}],"upstream_line":8677264,"upstream_offset":20913059253,"upstream_record_sha256":"731b97f9502bc06530a3f8c2fbc7743c373c04bca1ce261b11bc39459a4b9bb0","word":"antipalindromic"}
